## Review

**8 out of 10, Strong.** Presentation quality, judged with the SlideDeck Co review heuristic (https://slidedeck.co/review-methodology/). The ask, the evidence and the merit of the idea are not rated.

The deck has a clear route for an investor audience: safe harbor, company overview, platform, AI and agents, financials, guidance, long-term model, then an appendix. It has a consistent identity (orange to blue gradient, logo, footer bar, section dividers) and many slides with one subject, such as the big-number slides 16 and 17, the bar charts on 35 and 36, and the guidance cards on 41. Weaknesses are uneven density and legibility: the dense slides 13, 14, 18 and 39 use very small text, slide 38's line chart has no axis values, and slide 9 has a globe cropped to Africa and South America that adds little. Several pages (20, 22, 24, 30) have rough execution, such as a screenshot of a letter that cannot be read, a chart with tiny labels, and an overlapping 'IV' on slide 24.

**Worth keeping:** Keep the consistent gradient identity and section dividers, the clear bar charts with direct value labels on slides 35 and 36, the single-message big-number slides 16 and 17, and the colored component key on slide 38.

**Most useful repair:** Enlarge or simplify the tiny-text slides 13, 14, 18, 39 and 40, fix the overlapping 'IV' on slide 24, add axis values or labels to the slide 38 line chart, and replace the unreadable letter image on slide 20 with a legible pull quote.

**What would change the score:** I would cut or merge some of the AI and agent pages in the middle (19 to 25) so the argument reaches the financials faster, and rework slide 9 so the globe shows the full network.

_Confidence high. 48 of 48 pages inspected on contact sheets, 4 at readable size. Reviewed Reviewed 2026-11-08. Model-assisted review (claude-sonnet-5-5)._

## Summary

The deck is Cloudflare's investor presentation as of June 30, 2026, and opens with a safe harbor disclosure covering forward-looking statements and non-GAAP measures. It then moves to a company overview with a mission slide and an at-a-glance page of key figures such as large customers, revenue, and network reach. The middle sections describe a single programmable network and composable platform, how the company's product and network flywheels work, a growing total addressable market, and a case for AI and agents as a new demand driver, including a proposed market structure for metered agent activity. The financial section covers the business model, revenue and customer growth, dollar-based net retention, unit economics, operating expense leverage, cash position, Q3 and full-year guidance, and a long-term operating model with a GAAP profitability target. It closes with the appendix containing the GAAP to non-GAAP reconciliation, key business metric tables, and definitions and Gartner sources. Slides worth studying include the single-platform diagram on slide 9, the revenue and customer growth chart on slide 35, and the unit economics explanation on slide 38.

## Questions about this deck

**How many slides does the Cloudflare, Inc. investor presentation as of June 30, 2026 have?**

The deck has 48 pages in total. The Financial Overview section begins on slide 32.

**What does the Cloudflare, Inc. investor presentation say about its total addressable market?**

Slide 14 shows a growing TAM, with a total of $32B in 2018, $238B in 2026, $282B in 2027, $329B in 2028, and $384B in 2029. The slide notes that the figures reflect the markets addressed by Cloudflare's current product offerings and are based on Gartner research.

**What revenue and customer figures does Cloudflare, Inc. report in its at-a-glance page?**

Slide 5 shows total revenue of $512M for Q2 2025 and $696M for Q2 2026. The same slide lists 4,600+ Large Customers and 49% of revenue from outside of the U.S.

**What does the Cloudflare, Inc. deck say about growth in AI agent traffic?**

Slide 17 reports a 1,700%+ increase in daily AI agent requests on Cloudflare's network over one year. The chart compares June 1, 2025 with May 31, 2026, measured in billions of requests per day.

**Layout**

Left-aligned title slide. Logo sits top-left at about 8% from left, with a very large two-line title below it and a date line near the bottom. A thin vertical white rule runs down the left at about 5.6% from the left edge. Copyright footer sits at the bottom left, aligned with the rule.

**Visuals**

Full-bleed abstract gradient background image: blue at the bottom-left corner, shifting through purple and magenta to crimson red in the centre-left, then orange and amber toward the right. Translucent, soft-edged light-orange and yellow ribbon and wing shapes sweep diagonally from the bottom right up to the top-right corner, where they converge into a pale yellow glow. A thin vertical white line runs along the left. The white Cloudflare logo (cloud icon plus the wordmark CLOUDFLARE in wide spaced bold capitals) is at top left.

**Design**

Background: Full-bleed gradient from blue #2548E8 at bottom-left through purple and crimson #C8142C to orange #F97B2E and amber #FDA032 at right, with translucent diagonal ribbons. All text is white #FFFFFF. Typeface is Inter, a sans-serif: ExtraBold for the title at about 16% of slide height, Medium for the date at about 4%, Regular for the footer at about 1.2%. Chart 1 (Total Revenue, $M): five navy vertical bars with no y-axis, a thin grey baseline; bars 2023 $1,297, 2024 $1,670, 2025 $2,168, then after a vertical grey dashed divider Q2 2025 $512 and Q2 2026 $696. 6

**Layout**

White slide with a left-aligned title near the top and a Cloudflare logo at top right. Six stacked rounded-rectangle rows span the left ~89% width, each with a category icon and label in the left column and six company logos spaced evenly across the row. Copyright footer at bottom left, page number at bottom right, gradient bar along the bottom edge.

**Visuals**

Cloudflare logo (orange cloud with black spaced-capital wordmark) top right. Six rounded rows with coloured outlines: yellow-orange (Media), amber (Software & SaaS), orange (Retail & E-commerce), red-orange (Finance & Fintech), crimson (Gaming & Gambling), dark maroon (Healthcare). Category icons: a video/play window (Media), a cloud with up arrow (Software), a shopping cart (Retail), a document with bar chart (Finance), a game controller (Gaming), a heart with pulse line (Healthcare). Company logos in full colour, row 1: Sony Music red dot-matrix circle, Ziff Davis navy wordmark, Thomson Reuters orange dotted circle, reddit orange alien and wordmark, stack overflow, black AXIOS. Row 2: workday blue with orange arc, Discord blue-purple, Canva purple-teal circle, ORACLE red, IBM blue striped, BROADCOM black with red circle. Row 3: Carrefour red/blue, shopify green bag, Delivery Hero red, Uber black, DOORDASH red, FOSSIL oval badge. Row 4: SoFi teal, Allianz navy, VISA blue, AON red, WELLS FARGO red with yellow underline, ICE blue bracket with black text. Row 5: CCP grey dots, playtech navy, mod.io teal and black, SYBO white on blue, kaizen GAMING orange arrow, Caliente red script. Row 6: labcorp blue circle with grey text, Roche blue hexagon, Western Health green-purple leaf, VERTO navy, Fullscript with green heart, Doctolib navy script. Thin horizontal gradient bar at the bottom runs orange to crimson to deep purple to blue.

**Design**

Background: White #FFFFFF. Clean white background; title in black Inter SemiBold about 5.4% of slide height; category labels in bold Inter caps about 2.2% height, coloured to match each row outline; rounded rectangle outlines about 3px, large corner radius, no fill or shadow; footer small black Inter regular; page number black Inter SemiBold.

### Slide 7

**Text**

Cloudflare Is Built For What’s Next
One composable platform
Unified security
for external-facing / systems and internal / resources
Any-to-any / connectivity
for users, apps, branches, / data centers, & clouds
Flexibility
to customize the / platform with full-stack / developer tools
One programmable network
More effective
by simplifying / connectivity and / policy management
More productive
by ensuring fast, reliable, / and consistent user / experiences everywhere
More agile
by innovating rapidly to / meet evolving security / requirements
© 2026 Cloudflare, Inc.
7

**Layout**

Title top-left with the Cloudflare logo at top right. A globe illustration with a cloud sits on the left (about 3% to 43% wide). Two horizontal tinted bands sit on the right, each with a coloured heading and three text columns. Copyright bottom-left, page number bottom-right, gradient strip along the bottom edge.

**Visuals**

Left: a circular globe illustration with orange and peach latitude/longitude ellipses and a thin pale-blue outer ring. A large white cloud with a thick orange-to-peach gradient outline sits in the centre. Six circular peach-outlined icon badges sit around the globe, each with an orange line icon: top-left a shield with an arrow, top-right a route with nodes and a location pin, left a cursor arrow with sparkle rays, right a toggle switch with a check, bottom-left a rocket, bottom-right a lightning bolt with circular arrows. Small orange and yellow dots mark points along the rings. Right: an upper band with a horizontal gradient from white to pale peach (#FDE8CC) and an orange underline (#F6821F) at the bottom. A lower band with a gradient from white to pale blue (#E0ECFD) and a light-blue underline (#A9C8FA) at the bottom. Top-right: the Cloudflare logo, an orange cloud with the wordmark CLOUDFLARE in dark bold spaced capitals. Bottom edge: a thin horizontal gradient strip from orange (#F6A33A) through red-orange (#FF6633) and crimson (#C0143C) to dark navy (#150F6B) and bright blue (#3A6FF0).

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background. The title is black #000000 with the words 'What’s Next' in orange-red #FF6633, in Inter SemiBold at about 5.4% of slide height. The upper section heading is orange #F6821F and the lower one blue #448BFE, both Inter SemiBold at about 3.5%. Column headings are dark grey #222222 Inter SemiBold at about 3%. Body text is #222222 Inter Regular at about 2.5%. The footer is small Inter Regular at 1.2%. The page number is Inter SemiBold. No shadows or rounded corners on the bands, which have square edges.

8

**Layout**

Title in two lines at top left. Left column holds four stats, each with an orange icon at left and a big number plus unit with a description beneath. A world map fills the right-centre. A light blue band with three stat columns, separated by thin orange vertical lines, runs across the lower part. Footnote and copyright sit bottom left, page number bottom right, Cloudflare logo top right, gradient strip along the bottom edge.

**Visuals**

Cloudflare logo top right: an orange cloud mark with the spaced dark wordmark CLOUDFLARE. Four orange outline icons in the left column: a location pin beside 335+, a network node/mesh icon beside ~13,000, a hub-and-spoke node icon beside 534 Tbps, and a three-person group icon beside 4,600+. A thin orange leader line runs from the right of '335+ cities' horizontally, then angles down-right to point at the map. World map in orange-to-light-orange gradient (pale peach #FDBF70 at the top to deeper orange #F58220 toward the equator and south), with many small navy dots marking Cloudflare cities, densest in Europe, the eastern US and East Asia. Thin dark purple-grey curved lines link regions across the Atlantic, Pacific and over land. A white box at the bottom of the map holds the legend: a navy dot with 'Cloudflare city (as of Q2 2026)'. Two thin orange vertical divider lines at about x 34% and x 65.6% cross the blue band. A thin horizontal gradient bar at the very bottom runs from orange through red and deep navy to bright blue.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background. Title in black Inter, 'A Single, Programmable Network' SemiBold, 'Unlike Any Other' Light, about 5.4% of slide height. Stat numbers in bold orange #FF6633 (about 4.9% height) with smaller bold units (about 3%) and regular orange descriptions (about 2.2%). Bottom band is pale blue #DCE5F7 (#BACEF5 at 51% opacity) with dark navy #0F006B bold numbers (about 4.9%) and Roboto Regular navy descriptions (about 2.2%). Footnote is small Inter, grey #414142 and black. Flat styling, no shadows or rounded corners. Bottom gradient strip is about 2% of the height.

### Slide 9

**Review notes**

- Worth studying: A simple layered diagram shows that every service runs on every server in every data center, making the single-platform claim visible at a glance.
- Review: Weaknesses are uneven density and legibility: the dense slides 13, 14, 18 and 39 use very small text, slide 38's line chart has no axis values, and slide 9 has a globe cropped to Africa and South America that adds little.
- What would change the score: I would cut or merge some of the AI and agent pages in the middle (19 to 25) so the argument reaches the financials faster, and rework slide 9 so the globe shows the full network.

**Text**

A Single, Composable Platform
Of Internet-Native Services
Every
Cloudflare
service
Built to run over
every Cloudflare
server
In every Cloudflare
data center
Across 335+ cities
in 125+ countries
Note: Figures as of June 30, 2026.
© 2026 Cloudflare, Inc.
9

**Layout**

Title block top-left in two lines with the Cloudflare logo at top right. Four progressively larger circles run left to right along a horizontal centreline, joined by thin orange connector lines, each with a centred bold caption beneath it. Footnote text sits bottom-left, the page number bottom-right, and a gradient bar runs along the bottom edge.

**Visuals**

Four outlined circles of increasing size, connected by thin orange lines fanning out from one circle to the next. 1) Small circle with a pale peach (#FFE9CB) ring and white centre holding an orange network/node icon: five nodes joined by lines. 2) Medium circle with a thin orange outline containing an orange line-art illustration of two stacked server units, the lower one with a cloud outline. 3) Larger circle with an orange outline containing an orange line-art illustration of an office building with a data center server rack stack and an orange map pin at lower left. 4) Largest circle with a thin orange outline containing an orange-shaded globe showing South America, Africa and Europe in orange gradient (red-orange at the southern tips), with pale orange arcs connecting cities and many blue dots with light blue halos marking cities, densest over Europe. The Cloudflare logo, an orange cloud with the wordmark CLOUDFLARE in dark grey spaced capitals, sits top right. A thin horizontal gradient bar at the bottom edge runs from orange (#FBAD41) through red (#C8102E) and deep purple/navy to blue (#3B6FE8).

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background. Titles in black #000000 Inter, first line SemiBold and second line Light, about 5.4% of slide height. Captions in black Inter Bold about 2.2% of slide height. Footnotes in Inter Regular about 1.2%, grey #414142 for the note and black for the copyright. Accents are orange #F6821F and #FF6633 for circle outlines, with a gradient bar at the bottom. No shadows.

**Text**

Our Favorable Competitive Position
On-Premises
Point Solutions
Public Cloud
Limitations

10

**Layout**

Title at top left with Cloudflare logo at top right. Three equal columns separated by thin vertical divider lines; each has a pale circle with an orange icon, a coloured header banner across the circle's lower part, and two limitation items below, each with a grey-blue icon on the left and bold text on the right. A rotated 'Limitations' label sits at the far left. Copyright footer at bottom left, page number at bottom right, gradient bar along the bottom edge.

**Visuals**

Cloudflare logo (orange cloud with 'CLOUDFLARE' wordmark) top right. Three pale peach circles (#FFF3E6) over grey semicircle bases (#E7E6E6), each with an orange outline icon: a server rack (two stacked units), a monitor with bar chart over a small device (point solutions), and two overlapping clouds (public cloud). Banners in yellow-orange, orange and red-orange. Grey-blue line icons for limitations: dollar sign in circle; cloud with X; document with magnifying glass; circular arrows with X; pencil with X; chain link with padlock. Two thin vertical divider lines between columns (orange-to-grey). Bottom horizontal gradient bar from orange through crimson and deep navy to bright blue.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF with a gradient bar (orange to crimson to navy to blue) along the bottom edge. White background. Title black #000000 in Inter SemiBold, about 5.4% of slide height. Column headers white Inter Black about 3% on banners #FBAD41, #F6821F and #FF6633. Body text Inter Bold about 2.5% in dark grey #404041. 'Limitations' label Inter Bold 3.5% in #404243, rotated 90 degrees. Icons in slate blue-grey #4F6283 and orange #FF6633. Flat style, no shadows, banners are square-cornered rectangles.

11

**Layout**

Title at top left spanning most of the width, Cloudflare logo in the top right corner. A flywheel diagram of two interlocking elliptical loops fills the body: Serve Everyone at top centre, Global Scale at bottom centre, Better Products and Increased Efficiency on the left, Serverless Architecture and Ease of use on the right, central Cloudflare logo with tagline. Copyright footer at bottom left, page number at bottom right, gradient bar along the bottom edge.

**Visuals**

Flywheel diagram with curved arrows. Orange (#F6821F) arrows form the outer loop: from Better Products up and over to Serve Everyone (arrowhead at the top left of Serve Everyone), from Serve Everyone curving right/down through the 'Product' label to Ease of use (arrowhead pointing down), from Ease of use curving down and left to Global Scale, from Global Scale curving left and up to Better Products (arrowhead pointing up). Dark navy (#0F006B) arrows form the inner loop: from Increased Efficiency curving up to Serve Everyone (arrowhead at the top left), from Serve Everyone curving down through the 'Network' label to Serverless Architecture (arrowhead pointing down), from Serverless Architecture curving down to Global Scale (arrowhead pointing left at the globe), from Global Scale curving up-left to Increased Efficiency (arrowhead pointing up). Icons: orange outline group of three people above Serve Everyone; orange outline globe above Global Scale; red-orange browser window with an asterisk above Better Products; navy double chevron above Increased Efficiency; navy starburst/spark icon above Serverless Architecture; red-orange circle with a checkmark above Ease of use. Centre: Cloudflare cloud logo in orange with dark grey 'CLOUDFLARE' wordmark. Top right: small Cloudflare logo with orange cloud and dark 'CLOUDFLARE' wordmark. Bottom edge: thin horizontal gradient bar from orange through red and magenta to purple, navy and blue.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Clean white background. Title in black Inter SemiBold, large (about 5.4% of slide height). Node labels in black Inter Bold about 2.7% of slide height. 'Network' in navy #0F006B and 'Product' in orange #F6821F, Inter ExtraBold about 3.5%. Arrows are thick curved strokes with solid triangular heads, orange #F6821F and navy #0F006B. Footer text in small black Inter Regular. No shadows. Bottom gradient bar from #F6821F-ish orange via crimson #BE1238 to navy #10006B and blue #3B6BF0.

12

**Layout**

White slide with the title top-left and a Cloudflare logo top-right. A large rounded navy-outlined container fills the central area, holding two side-by-side cream-bordered panels above one full-width cream-bordered panel, with a navy base bar at the bottom. Footer copyright at bottom-left, page number bottom-right, gradient strip along the bottom edge.

**Visuals**

Cloudflare logo (orange cloud with black spaced 'CLOUDFLARE' wordmark) at top right. A large rounded rectangle with a thick navy outline and pale lavender fill contains: a left panel (cream border, white inside, orange outline) with an orange bar 'Cloudflare Network Services' with a network-node icon and a red-orange bar 'Cloudflare Application Services' with a rocket icon; a right panel with a light orange bar 'Cloudflare Zero Trust Services' with a shield-arrow icon. The orange and red-orange bars extend rightward beyond the left panel border, overlapping the gap and the right panel's left edge up to about x=61%. A bottom panel has a maroon bar 'Cloudflare Developer Platform' with a code-brackets icon. A dark navy bar at the bottom of the container has a white globe icon between 'One Platform' and 'One Network'. A horizontal gradient strip at the slide bottom runs from orange through red and dark purple to blue.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF with a bottom gradient strip from orange #F9A03A through red #C41230 and dark purple to blue #3A6FF0. White background, black semibold Inter title with the italic word 'Everywhere' in orange #FF6633. Panel headings in bold orange #FF6633 with semibold black second lines. Bars: orange #F6821F, red-orange #FF6633, light orange #FBAD41, maroon #960C3E, navy #0F006B with white semibold text. Panels have cream #FBE8CC borders with thin orange outlines and large rounded corners about 2% of height. Outer container has lavender #E0E6FF fill with a navy #0F006B outline. Typeface Inter sans-serif throughout. Title about 5.4% of slide height, headings about 3.3%, bar text about 2.8%.

### Slide 13

**Review notes**

- Review: Weaknesses are uneven density and legibility: the dense slides 13, 14, 18 and 39 use very small text, slide 38's line chart has no axis values, and slide 9 has a globe cropped to Africa and South America that adds little.
- Suggested repair: Enlarge or simplify the tiny-text slides 13, 14, 18, 39 and 40, fix the overlapping 'IV' on slide 24, add axis values or labels to the slide 38 line chart, and replace the unreadable letter image on slide 20 with a legible pull quote.

**Layout**

Title at top left with the Cloudflare logo at top right. A legend row of five circular category icons with labels sits below the title. A horizontal timeline runs across the lower-middle of the slide (about 61% down) with year markers 2010, 2014, 2018, 2022 and 2024 and vertical tick lines. Product icons with labels are stacked in columns above the line for products launched in the preceding interval and below the line for the next. Footnote and copyright at bottom left, slide number at bottom right, gradient bar along the bottom edge.

**Visuals**

Cloudflare logo (orange cloud with black spaced wordmark CLOUDFLARE) at top right. Legend of five filled circles with white line icons: crimson circle with a server/hexagon-pin icon (Application Services), amber circle with a shield and arrow (Zero Trust Services), orange-red circle with a node network icon (Network Services), navy circle with a code-chevron diamond icon (Developer Services), green circle with a credit card icon (PAYGO). A horizontal black timeline with four pale peach dots (2010 to 2014 area) and then orange dots at regular intervals (about every 6.4% of width) to the right edge; vertical black lines mark 2014 (up), 2018 (down), 2022 (up) and 2024 (down). About 80 small line-art product icons, each colour-coded by category (crimson #9B1B4B for Application Services, amber #F6A33B for Zero Trust, orange #F26B2A for Network, navy #1A1A7A for Developer, green #2E9D6A for PAYGO/Page Rules and Automatic Platform Optimization) with a bold label beneath each. Full-width gradient strip at the bottom from orange to red to navy to blue.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF with a 2% tall horizontal gradient bar (orange to red to navy to blue) along the bottom edge. White background. Title in black semi-bold Inter sans-serif, about 5.4% of slide height. Year labels in black extra-bold Inter at about 2.5% height. Product labels black semi-bold Inter about 1% height, centered under icons. Legend labels black semi-bold Inter about 1.6%. Footnote grey #414142 and copyright black regular Inter about 1.2%. Icons are thin outline line art. Bottom 2% gradient bar from orange #F6821F through red #C8102E and deep navy #1A1A6E to blue #3B6BF5. No shadows.

### Slide 14

**Review notes**

- Review: Weaknesses are uneven density and legibility: the dense slides 13, 14, 18 and 39 use very small text, slide 38's line chart has no axis values, and slide 9 has a globe cropped to Africa and South America that adds little.
- Suggested repair: Enlarge or simplify the tiny-text slides 13, 14, 18, 39 and 40, fix the overlapping 'IV' on slide 24, add axis values or labels to the slide 38 line chart, and replace the unreadable letter image on slide 20 with a legible pull quote.

14

**Layout**

White slide with title top-left and Cloudflare logo top-right. Left-centre is a stacked bar chart of five columns (2018, 2026-2029) with category callouts: Application Services on the left, Developer, Network and Zero Trust lists in a column to the right of the bars. A gradient panel on the right edge holds a list of growth areas. Footer has copyright at bottom left, note text in the bottom centre-left and page number bottom right.

**Visuals**

Stacked bar chart with no y-axis, on a light grey baseline at about y 87.5%. Five columns labelled 2018, 2026, 2027, 2028, 2029 with totals above: $32B, $238B, $282B, $329B, $384B. Each bar is stacked from bottom to top: maroon (Application Services) with a white hexagon server-pin icon; orange (Network) with white network-node icon; amber (Zero Trust) with white shield-arrow icon; dark navy (Developer) with white code-chevron icon. 2018 has only the maroon segment (about $32B). In 2026-2029, the orange and amber segments are enclosed by a blue rounded-corner outline (#3E74FF). A dashed light-blue vertical line separates 2018 from 2026. A blue hexagon with the number 1 and the label Cloudflare One sits at the left with a blue line running to the 2026 blue box. Section header underlines are navy (Developer), orange (Network), amber (Zero Trust), and maroon (Application Services); an orange line connects the Network section to the 2029 box and a maroon line connects Application Services to the 2018 bar. The right panel has a rounded top-left corner and a gradient from deep purple through crimson and orange to amber, with an orange horizontal rule below the heading. Icons in white: Act IV (circle with arrows), Database (cylinder stack), Internet of Things (circle with dot), 5G Cellular (antenna tower), Payments (document with bars). Cloudflare logo: orange cloud with CLOUDFLARE wordmark in dark navy.

**Design**

Background: White #FFFFFF. White background; black bold Inter titles; section headings in bold navy #0F006B, orange #FF6633, amber #FB970E and maroon #960C3E. Body bullets in Inter Regular black with coloured bullets matching the section. Inter sans-serif throughout; title about 5.4% of slide height, body about 1.7%. Flat shapes, no shadows. Right panel gradient #2A0A6B to #C4143A to #FF6633 to #FBAD41.

### Slide 15

**Text**

AI and
agents
change
everything
© 2026 Cloudflare, Inc.
15

**Layout**

Full-bleed gradient background. Large four-line left-aligned headline sits at the left, vertically centred (x about 9%, y 26-72%). An isometric globe illustration occupies the right half. Cloudflare logo at top right, a thin horizontal line runs across the lower part from about x 22% to the right edge, copyright footer at bottom left, slide number at bottom right.

**Visuals**

Full-bleed diagonal gradient from deep indigo/purple at top left through crimson red to bright orange and amber at bottom right. Isometric illustration on the right: a translucent glowing crystal globe showing Earth continents (Americas, Africa, Europe) in orange and cream-white, resting on a white circular pedestal with a purple/orange rim, on a square white slab with orange outline edges. Cream four-pointed sparkle stars sit around the globe: a large one upper left of the globe, small ones on the globe, a small orange one at the right, plus a small cream diamond at the upper right. A soft white-orange glow flare sits at the bottom left (about x 13-25%, y 80-95%) at the start of a thin cream horizontal line that runs to the right edge at about y 87.7%. Cloudflare logo (white cloud icon with spaced capital wordmark) top right.

**Design**

Background gradient from indigo #1B0A6B at top left via crimson #C01632 to orange #FF6633 and amber #FBAD41 at bottom right. Headline in Inter SemiBold, about 10.9% of slide height, white #FFFFFF with the word 'change' in amber #FBAD41. Footer text Inter Regular about 1.2% in white, slide number Inter SemiBold about 2% in white. Thin 1px cream line, glow effects, no shadows on text.

### Slide 16

**Review notes**

- Review: It has a consistent identity (orange to blue gradient, logo, footer bar, section dividers) and many slides with one subject, such as the big-number slides 16 and 17, the bar charts on 35 and 36, and the guidance cards on 41.
- Worth keeping: Keep the consistent gradient identity and section dividers, the clear bar charts with direct value labels on slides 35 and 36, the single-message big-number slides 16 and 17, and the colored component key on slide 38.

**Text**

The Web Is Shifting
50%+
of traffic on Cloudflare's network / is now non-human
© 2026 Cloudflare, Inc.
16
CLOUDFLARE

**Layout**

White slide with the title top-left and a huge orange '50%+' statistic on the left half, with a two-line caption below it. A 4-column by 3-row grid of robot illustrations fills the right half. The Cloudflare logo sits top-right, the copyright is bottom-left, the page number is bottom-right, and a gradient strip runs along the bottom edge.

**Visuals**

A 4x3 grid of twelve line-art robot mascots in orange, amber, blue and red-pink outlines with pale cream fills. Each robot has round glasses, a boxy head and a body, and floats over stacked elliptical rings. Each holds or wears a different prop. Row 1: a headset robot holding a tool and a tablet; an orange robot with a steaming pot; a blue robot holding a cube; an orange robot with a lightbulb above its head. Row 2: a chef-hat robot with a spatula; a blue robot with a claw arm; a red-headed robot with a magnifying glass; an amber robot with a clipboard and pencil. Row 3: a cowboy-hat robot with sunglasses and a sheriff star; an orange robot with a wrench; an orange robot with a phone and a chat bubble; a blue headset robot with a clipboard. Top-right: Cloudflare logo with an orange cloud icon and the wordmark 'CLOUDFLARE' in dark wide bold caps. Bottom edge: a thin horizontal gradient bar running from orange-amber through red and deep purple-navy to bright blue.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Clean white background. The title is black #000000 with the 'Is Shifting' part in orange #FF6633 bold italic. The large stat is orange #FF6633 in Inter ExtraBold, with a very large '50' and a smaller '%+'. The caption is black Inter Light, with a size of about 5% of the slide height. The title is Inter SemiBold at about 5.4% of the slide height. The footer is small Inter Regular. There are no shadows. The illustrations use thin outline strokes.

White slide with the title top-left and the Cloudflare logo top-right. The left half holds a chart with a heading and an orange rule above it. The right half holds a gradient banner bleeding off the right edge, with centered text beneath it. The copyright sits bottom-left, the page number bottom-right, and a gradient strip runs along the bottom edge.

**Visuals**

Left: a bar chart with two vertical bars of the same light orange (#F9AB41) on a white background, with a thin grey baseline. The left bar, June 1, 2025, is very short, about 2.5% of the slide height. The right bar, May 31, 2026, is tall, about 43% of the slide height, roughly 17 times the left bar. The bars have no printed values, no y-axis and no gridlines. The category labels are in small bold black text under each bar. A thin orange rule (#F68220) runs under the chart heading. Right: a rounded-left rectangle banner, with its left corners rounded to about 4% of the slide width and its right edge bleeding off the slide, filled with a horizontal gradient from orange (#F6821F) through bright orange-red (#FF6633) to crimson (#C41A33). It holds the large white text 1,700%+. Top right: the Cloudflare logo, an orange cloud icon with the wordmark CLOUDFLARE in dark letter-spaced bold caps. Bottom: a full-width thin gradient strip from orange (#F9AB41) through red (#C41230) and deep purple/navy (#14106B) to blue (#3B6FE8).

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background. The title is black (#000000) Inter SemiBold at about 5.4% of the slide height, with 'Is Real' in orange-red (#FF6633) Inter SemiBold Italic. The chart heading is black Inter Bold plus Inter Light at about 3.2%. The banner number is white Inter Black at about 11.8%. INCREASE is orange-red (#FF6633) Inter Bold at about 6%. The caption is black Inter Light at about 4.6%, centered. The footer text is small black Inter Regular at about 1.2%. The page number is Inter SemiBold at about 2%. There are no shadows. The banner uses a horizontal gradient and rounded corners.

### Slide 18

**Review notes**

- Review: Weaknesses are uneven density and legibility: the dense slides 13, 14, 18 and 39 use very small text, slide 38's line chart has no axis values, and slide 9 has a globe cropped to Africa and South America that adds little.
- Suggested repair: Enlarge or simplify the tiny-text slides 13, 14, 18, 39 and 40, fix the overlapping 'IV' on slide 24, add axis values or labels to the slide 38 line chart, and replace the unreadable letter image on slide 20 with a legible pull quote.

**Layout**

Title in two lines top-left with the Cloudflare logo at top right. A large matrix occupies the left-centre (x 12–76%): a gradient Cloudflare banner on top, four columns of category cards, rows of vendor logos beneath blue headers, and an advantages bar at the bottom. Row labels sit in the left column in orange or black. A right column holds the 'We offer AI security…' statement and two badges at the bottom right. Footer copyright bottom-left, page number bottom-right, gradient strip along the bottom edge.

**Visuals**

Cloudflare orange cloud logo with wordmark top right. Wide banner with yellow-to-orange-to-crimson gradient and a white Cloudflare cloud logo with wordmark in the centre. Four category cards with coloured header tabs (amber, orange, vermilion, crimson) over cream bodies. Blue header bars for vendor groups. Vendor logos in grey/black/colour: zscaler, Cisco, Palo Alto Networks, Fortinet, netskope, CATO Networks, docker, GitHub, aws, Azure, Akamai, F5, fastly, Google Cloud, Obsidian, Nightfall AI, Lasso Security, aembit, Operant, AIM Security, CalypsoAI, Vercel. Bottom advantages bar with an orange gradient frame and a cream/white interior. Orange badge with a shield-check icon, crimson badge with a wrench icon. A horizontal gradient strip along the bottom edge from orange through red and dark purple to blue.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF with a thin orange-to-red-to-purple-to-blue gradient strip along the bottom edge. White background. Title in Inter Light black #000000 with 'Only Vendor' in Inter SemiBold and the second line in orange #FF6633 SemiBold, about 5.4% of slide height. Row labels in Inter Bold orange #FF6633 or black. Card headers in white Inter SemiBold; card body text black Inter Regular. Blue #3E74FF header bars with white text. Right statement in Inter Light/Bold at 3.7% height with 'and' in orange. Rounded corners on cards and badges, thin orange outlines on cards. Gradient banner and bottom strip.

### Slide 20

**Review notes**

- Suggested repair: Enlarge or simplify the tiny-text slides 13, 14, 18, 39 and 40, fix the overlapping 'IV' on slide 24, add axis values or labels to the slide 38 line chart, and replace the unreadable letter image on slide 20 with a legible pull quote.

**Text**

Andy Jassy Was Right About Primitives...
2023 Letter to / Shareholders
What matters to builders is having the right tools to keep rapidly improving customer experiences.
The best way we know how to do this is by building primitive services. Think of them as discrete, foundational building blocks that builders can weave together in whatever combination they desire. Here's how we described primitives in our 2003 AWS Vision document:
"Primitives are the raw parts or the most foundational-level building blocks for software developers. They're indivisible (if they can be functionally split into two they must) and they do one thing really well. They're meant to be used together rather than as solutions in and of themselves. And, we'll build them for maximum developer flexibility. We won't put a bunch of constraints on primitives to guard against developers hurting themselves. Rather, we'll optimize for developer freedom and innovation."
Of course, this concept of primitives can be applied to more than software development, but they're especially relevant in technology. And, over the last 20 years, primitives have been at the heart of how we've innovated quickly.
Pursuing primitives is not a guarantee of success. There are many you could build, and even more ways to combine them. But, a good compass is to pick real customer problems you're trying to solve.
"The best way we know how to do this is by building primitive services.
...
Primitives are the raw parts or the most foundational-level building blocks for software developers.
...
And, over the last 20 years, primitives have been at the heart of how we've innovated quickly.
...
Our AWS primitive services were designed from the start to be different.
...
Primitives, done well, rapidly accelerate builders' ability to innovate.
...
a good compass is to pick real customer problems you're trying to solve."
© 2026 Cloudflare, Inc.
20
CLOUDFLARE

**Layout**

Title at top left with Cloudflare logo at top right. Left half holds two overlapping letter-document screenshots (front page lower-left, back page offset to the right and below); right column holds a left-aligned quote in ellipsis-separated fragments. Footer copyright at bottom left, page number bottom right, gradient bar along the bottom edge.

**Visuals**

Two overlapping document screenshots with thin orange (#FF6633) borders. Front: a dark navy header band with white text '2023 Letter to Shareholders' and an orange Amazon smile arrow, a zigzag line, then small black body text on white. Behind and to the right: a second page cropped by the front page, showing partially hidden text fragments and a zigzag line, with text at its bottom. A thin orange straight line runs diagonally from the front page body text (at the words 'primitive services') up to the top-left of the quote column. The Cloudflare logo (orange cloud with black wordmark) at top right. A thin horizontal gradient bar at the bottom runs from orange through red and purple to navy to blue.

**Design**

Background: White #FFFFFF with a thin multicolor gradient bar along the bottom edge. White background. Title in Inter SemiBold, black #000000 with the phrase 'Right About Primitives' in orange #FF6633, about 5.4% of slide height. Quote text in Inter Regular, dark gray #2F3033, about 3.2% height, with bold and italic emphasis. Orange outline and thin line accents; no shadows aside from subtle soft shadow on the pages. Bottom gradient bar orange #F9A03F to crimson #C8102E to navy #1A1060 to blue #3366E0.

**Layout**

Title at top left over a large line chart occupying the left ~63% of the slide. Right column holds a thin orange rule, a giant '7.4+' and 'MILLION' stat with a two-line caption, left-aligned. Cloudflare logo top right, footnote and copyright bottom left, page number bottom right, gradient bar along the bottom edge.

**Visuals**

Line chart with a thick orange (#FF6633) line with round markers, rising from near zero in 2019-2020 to about 7.4 million in June 2026, growing steeply. X axis labelled 2019 to 2026 in bold black, with a faint grey baseline and faint illegible grey tick labels; the faint y-axis labels at the left are nearly invisible. Nine data points are labelled in orange callouts with thin light-grey leader lines: Nov 2021 350,000+; May 2022 450,000+; Nov 2022 1+ million; Apr 2024 2+ million; Jun 2024 2.4+ million; Dec 2024 3+ million; Dec 2025 4.5+ million; Mar 2026 5.5+ million; Jun 2026 7.4+ million. A thin gradient strip at the bottom edge goes from orange through red and deep purple/navy to blue.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF with a thin orange-to-blue gradient bar along the bottom edge. White background. Title in black #000000 Inter SemiBold, about 5.4% of slide height. Big stat in orange #FF6633 Inter ExtraBold (7.4+ about 23.7%, MILLION about 12.8%). Caption in black Inter Regular about 5.9%. Chart callouts in orange #F6821F, bold date lines and regular value lines at about 2.1%. Axis year labels Inter Bold about 2.5% in black. Footnote Inter Regular about 1.2% in grey #414142 and black. Page number Inter SemiBold black. No shadows; flat styling.

23

**Layout**

Two equal rounded panels side by side (blue left, orange right) with a small illustration straddling each panel's top edge. Each panel has a centered two-line heading, a thin divider rule, then rows of company logo, arrow, destination logo. Cloudflare logo sits top right, copyright footer bottom left, page number bottom right, and a gradient bar runs along the bottom edge.

**Visuals**

Left panel: phone illustration in blue on top edge. Three rows pair airbnb (coral logo), Uber (black wordmark) and NETFLIX (red wordmark) with black arrows to the aws logo (black text, orange smile arrow). Right panel: orange robot mascot with glasses, holding a clipboard, on top edge. Four rows pair Figma (multicolour logo), Lovable (gradient heart icon with black wordmark), ANTHROP\C (black wordmark) and OpenAI (black knot logo with wordmark) with orange arrows to the orange Cloudflare cloud logo. Top-right Cloudflare cloud logo with CLOUDFLARE wordmark. Bottom gradient bar runs from orange through red and deep purple to bright blue.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White slide. Left panel has a pale blue gradient fill (#E6F0FF to white) with a 2px blue outline (#4A7CF0) and large corner radius. Right panel has a pale peach gradient fill (#FDE8D0 to white) with a 2px orange outline (#FF6633). Left heading is dark grey #222222 and right heading is red-orange #EC5140, both Inter SemiBold at about 4.4% of slide height. Dividers are thin lines in the panel colour. Arrows are black on the left and orange #FF6633 on the right. Footer text is Inter Regular, tiny and black. The bottom bar is an orange-to-red-to-navy-to-blue gradient.

Build

**Layout**

Full-bleed canyon photo. A large white equation headline spans the upper-middle of the slide and wraps, leaving 'IV' alone on a second line at centre. Three white icons with bold captions sit in a row below, at left, centre and right. The Cloudflare logo is in the top right, the copyright line at bottom left and the slide number at bottom right.

**Visuals**

Full-bleed photograph of a sandstone slot canyon with smooth, flowing, layered walls in saturated orange, coral and red-orange. It is lit warmly, with lighter yellow-orange highlights at the top right and darker red-brown curves at the left and centre. A warm orange tint sits over the photo. Three white line icons: a circular arrow with a check mark (left), a brick wall with a down arrow into a box (centre), and a robot head with an antenna and an asterisk (right). A white Cloudflare logo with cloud mark and wordmark 'CLOUDFLARE' is at top right. The 'IV' overlaps the top of the centre icon.

**Design**

Background is the orange canyon photo (dominant #F26B3A, with darker #C4533A and highlights #FDAA3E). Title text is white #FFFFFF in Inter ExtraBold at about 14% of slide height, with '+' and '=' in Inter Bold. Captions are white Inter Bold at about 4.2% of slide height. The footer and slide number are black #000000 in Inter, small. Icons are white line style with rounded strokes. No shadows or gradients except the tint.

**Layout**

Three columns: Supply on the left, Technical Capabilities centered, Demand on the right. Each column has an icon at top, an orange heading, and a list below. The left and right columns are flanked by faint cream chevron-shaped panels pointing inward, with thin orange double-line chevron outlines. A full-width gradient banner with a two-line centered white statement runs along the bottom. Copyright is at bottom left, slide number at bottom right, and the Cloudflare logo at top right.

**Visuals**

Orange line icons: a group of three people above Supply; a globe with person above Demand; a circular arrows/globe-with-crossed-arrows icon in a pale peach circle above Technical Capabilities. Small orange line icons beside list items: robot head (Bot Management), browser window with small robot (Web Bot Auth), document with keyboard (Content optimization), stacked server units (Infrastructure), wrench (Developer Tools), sparkle line chart (Visualization). Two pale cream gradient chevron panels with thin orange double-line chevron borders on each side of the centre column. Bottom banner gradient from orange through red and deep purple/navy to blue. Cloudflare logo with orange cloud at top right.

**Design**

Background: White #FFFFFF with pale cream #FFF4E9 chevron panels fading to white on the left and right; bottom banner gradient orange #F6821F to red #C4153A to navy #0F0A6B to blue #3366EE. White background with pale cream-peach chevron panels. Headings in bold orange #FF6633 Inter ExtraBold. Item titles in dark brown #521000 Inter SemiBold. Descriptions in muted brown #7A5A4A Inter SemiBold. Center capability items and subheadings in orange #FF6633 Inter SemiBold. Banner text white Inter Bold. Thin orange 2px lines for chevron outlines, no shadows.

26
CLOUDFLARE

**Layout**

White slide with the Cloudflare logo at top right and a left-aligned title at top left. Two pale peach horizontal bands span the full width at about 24% and 82% height. Between them are four centred columns, each with an orange heading, an illustration and a bold caption, separated by thin orange vertical lines. Footer notes sit at bottom left, the page number at bottom right, and a gradient bar runs along the bottom edge.

**Visuals**

Cloudflare logo (orange cloud icon with dark grey spaced-capital wordmark 'CLOUDFLARE') at top right. Four isometric orange-line illustrations: a globe of the Americas in peach/orange tones with orbit rings and small coloured dots; an orange stepped box with a lightbulb icon on top and a ladder leaning on its right side; an isometric city of orange outlined buildings with a small light-blue cube, orange blocks and green trees; a glass sphere with a green sprout on a translucent blue cube with orange ring, green leaves, a small orange cube and sparkles. Three thin orange vertical divider lines between the columns. Two pale peach horizontal gradient bands fading from left peach to white at the right. A thin gradient bar along the bottom edge runs from orange through red and deep purple/navy to blue.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background. Black title in Inter SemiBold at about 5.4% of slide height. Column headings in bold Inter, orange #FF6633, about 4.2%. The first 39 icons (rows 1-3 fully, row 4 columns 1-9... in total 39 counting 10+10+10+9) are outlined bulbs with a pale peach circular background and radiating rays. Their colour runs in a gradient from crimson-red at the bottom left (row 4, column 1) through orange-red to orange and light amber at the top right. The last icon in row 4 (column 10) is a pale blue outline bulb in a thin grey-bordered square. All 10 bulbs in row 5 are pale light-blue outline bulbs with no background circle. A thin horizontal gradient bar at the bottom edge runs from orange-yellow on the left through orange, crimson and deep purple to navy, then bright blue at the far right.

'run on Cloudflare' in Inter Light orange at 4.2%. Footnote text in Inter Regular, charcoal #414142 and black, about 1.2% of slide height. Six thin coloured leader lines with round dots connect the labels to the ring: payment system at top left, reliable scarcity at top right, analytics at right, value discovery at bottom right, streamlined workflows at bottom left, neutrality at left. The Cloudflare logo at top right is an orange cloud with the word CLOUDFLARE in dark grey wide-spaced bold capitals. A thin horizontal gradient bar at the bottom edge runs from yellow-orange through orange-red, crimson and deep navy to bright blue.

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background. The title's first part is orange #FF6633 and the second part is black #000000, in Inter SemiBold at about 5.4% of slide height. **Layout**

Title top-left with a Cloudflare logo at top right; subtitle beneath. A row of six equal rounded process cards with small orange icons above each and orange chevrons between them. Below, a pale peach band holds four white rounded tiles in a row, then a centered two-line orange statement. Copyright bottom-left, page number bottom-right, gradient strip along the bottom edge.

**Visuals**

Cloudflare logo (orange cloud with 'CLOUDFLARE' wordmark) at top right. Six orange line icons above the cards: a drafting compass, a magnifying glass, a sparkle/star burst, a circular arrows with check mark, a person outline, a credit card. Five orange chevron arrows between the cards pointing right. Six white cards with orange rounded outlines. Four white rounded tiles with crimson line icons: a dollar sign in a circle, a shield, a speed-lines/arrow routing glyph, a radiating burst. A thin orange line at the top of the peach band. A horizontal gradient bar at the bottom running orange to red to magenta to dark navy to blue.

**Design**

Background: White #FFFFFF with a pale peach #FFF4E9 band across the lower 40% and a thin orange-to-navy gradient strip at the bottom edge. White upper background with a pale peach (#FFF4E9) lower band. Title in dark brown-black #1A0A00 with 'Metered Event' in orange #FF6633, Inter SemiBold about 5.4% of slide height. Subtitle Inter Regular black about 3.7%. Card headings Inter Bold 2.7% in #1A0A00, card subtext Inter Regular 2.2%. Tile labels Inter Bold 2.7% in crimson #971145. Closing statement Inter Bold 3.75% in orange #FF6633. Cards have 2px orange #FF6633 outlines with large corner radii (~4% of height); tiles have white fill with about 2% corner radius and no shadow.

**Layout**

Left two thirds hold a centred two-line title and a bar chart with a title above it. A right-hand panel (about 32% wide, full height) holds the Cloudflare logo at top and four icon-plus-text rows separated by thin yellow lines. Copyright at bottom left, slide number at bottom right.

**Visuals**

Bar chart 'Total Revenue ($M)' with eight dark navy bars on a light grey baseline, no y-axis. Annual bars: 2020 $431, 2021 $656, 2022 $975, 2023 $1,297, 2024 $1,670, 2025 $2,168. A dotted vertical grey line separates them from the quarterly bars: Q2 2025 $512 and Q2 2026 $696. Value labels sit above each bar in dark grey bold. A large navy arrow rises diagonally from near the 2020 bar to above the 2025 bar, labelled '38% CAGR' rotated along it. A short navy arrow rises from above Q2 2025 to Q2 2026, labelled '36%' rotated. The right panel has a Cloudflare cloud logo with wordmark in white at the top right. Four white line icons: a hand holding an asterisk, a chart window with a check badge, a small square expanding into a larger one with an arrow, and stacked browser windows with a lightning bolt. Three thin yellow horizontal dividers lie between the rows.

**Design**

Background: White #FFFFFF with a right panel of purple-to-red-to-orange gradient. White left background. The right panel has a diagonal gradient from deep purple #1A0066 at top right, through magenta/crimson #C01535, to orange #F58220 and light orange #FBA640 at bottom left. Title is black #000000 Inter SemiBold, about 5.4% of slide height. Chart labels and axis are #414142 Inter Bold, about 2.8%. Chart title is #222222 Inter Bold at 3.7%. Bars and arrows are #0F006B. Panel text is white Inter Bold at 3.2%. Dividers are yellow #FFC72C at about 0.3% thick. No shadows or rounded corners.

### Slide 38

**Review notes**

- Worth studying: Each term in the unit economics formula is defined in its own labeled box beside the equation, which makes a dense calculation easy to follow.
- Review: Weaknesses are uneven density and legibility: the dense slides 13, 14, 18 and 39 use very small text, slide 38's line chart has no axis values, and slide 9 has a globe cropped to Africa and South America that adds little.
- Worth keeping: Keep the consistent gradient identity and section dividers, the clear bar charts with direct value labels on slides 35 and 36, the single-message big-number slides 16 and 17, and the colored component key on slide 38.
- Suggested repair: Enlarge or simplify the tiny-text slides 13, 14, 18, 39 and 40, fix the overlapping 'IV' on slide 24, add axis values or labels to the slide 38 line chart, and replace the unreadable letter image on slide 20 with a legible pull quote.

**Text**

Robust Unit Economics
Cost to Book / (CTB)
What does it “cost to book” $1 of / incremental ARR? CTB = Sales & / Marketing cost per dollar of / incremental ARR
Cost to Serve / (CTS)
How much does it “cost to serve” / the lifetime revenue? CTS = / Delivery, R&D, Support & G&A cost / as % of ARR
Attrition
What’s the expected lifetime / revenue from $1 ARR? Lifetime / Revenue (LTR) = $1 ARR / attrition / rate
Unit Economic / Margin
What is the economic margin created / by $1 ARR? Unit Economic Margin = / [LTR - CTB - (CTS ∗ LTR) ] / LTR
Unit Economic Margin by Component
44 %
Unit / Economic / Margin
2019 2020 2021 2022 2023 2024 2025
Cost To Book Cost To Serve Attrition Unit Economic Margin
Note: Unit economic margin in this and subsequent slides are calculations based on assumptions derived from Cloudflare’s historical operations regarding unit lifetime revenue and subscription economics, including cost to book, cost to serve, and attrition dynamics.
Unit economic margin is not operating margin. The calculations charted are as of fourth quarter of the respective years.
© 2026 Cloudflare, Inc.
38

**Layout**

Title top left with Cloudflare logo at top right. Left column (about 3–47% wide) has a cream panel with three pill labels on the left and definition text on the right, then an orange divider line and a fourth pill with definition below. Right column holds a chart title with an orange underline and a line chart. Footnote and copyright at bottom left, page number at bottom right, gradient bar along the bottom edge.

**Visuals**

Cloudflare logo (orange cloud icon with dark grey spaced 'CLOUDFLARE' wordmark) at top right. Four rounded pill shapes: navy Cost to Book, crimson Cost to Serve, bright blue Attrition, orange Unit Economic Margin. Thin orange hand-drawn-style horizontal line below cream panel. Smooth line chart with four series over 2019–2025 on a white background with a grey baseline axis and no gridlines or y-axis labels. Cost To Book (navy) starts high at about 2019, dips around 2021, rises to a peak in 2024, and falls in 2025. Cost To Serve (crimson) starts mid-high and declines gradually, flattening toward 2025 and meeting the orange line. Attrition (blue) is a flat low line with a slight dip around 2022. Unit Economic Margin (orange) rises from the lowest point in 2019 to a plateau from 2021 and ends in 2025, with round dot markers at each year. An orange circle callout at upper right of the chart reads 44% Unit Economic Margin. Legend below the axis uses a navy dash, crimson dash, blue dash and orange dot. Orange-to-red-to-purple-to-blue gradient strip along the bottom edge.

**Design**

Background: White #FFFFFF with a thin orange-to-crimson-to-navy-to-blue gradient strip along the bottom edge. White background. Title black #000000 in Inter SemiBold, about 5.4% of slide height. Chart title dark grey #222222 Inter Bold. Body text black Inter, bold lead-in questions with regular continuation, about 2.5% of height. Pill labels white Inter Bold on navy #0F006B, crimson #960C3E, blue #3E74FF and orange #FBAD41 fills with fully rounded ends. Cream panel #FFF1DB. Accent orange #F6821F. Footnote grey #414142 in tiny Inter. No shadows.

**Layout**

White slide with left-aligned title at top-left and Cloudflare logo at top-right. A centered chart subtitle sits below, followed by a six-column stacked bar chart spanning the width, a legend row beneath, footnotes at bottom-left, page number at bottom-right and a gradient strip along the bottom edge.

**Visuals**

Stacked column chart titled 'Non-GAAP Operating Expenses & Operating Margin (% of Revenue)'. Years on top: 2021, 2022, 2023, 2024, 2025, 2026E*. A thin light-grey horizontal baseline at about y 48% runs across the chart. For each year (except 2026E) a cost stack hangs downward from the baseline: COGS (dark navy), S&M (light orange), R&D (orange), G&A (crimson). Values 2021: COGS 21%, S&M 45%, R&D 20%, G&A 15%; 2022: 22%, 42%, 19%, 14%; 2023: 22%, 40%, 17%, 12%; 2024: 21%, 38%, 16%, 11%; 2025: 24%, 36%, 16%, 10%. Operating margin bars (bright blue) sit just right of each stack rising above the baseline: 2021 -1% (dashed outline, light-blue fill, small bar below baseline), 2022 4%, 2023 9%, 2024 14%, 2025 14%, 2026E 16% (no cost stack). Value labels are white on navy, orange and crimson segments, dark blue on S&M, blue above margin bars. Legend row with square swatches: COGS navy, S&M light orange, R&D orange, G&A crimson, Operating Margin blue. Cloudflare logo (orange cloud with CLOUDFLARE wordmark) top-right. Gradient bar along bottom edge from orange through red and crimson to dark purple and blue.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF with a thin orange-to-red-to-purple-to-blue gradient strip along the bottom edge. Clean white background. Title black #000000 Inter SemiBold about 5.4% of slide height; chart title and year labels black Inter Bold; data labels Inter Bold about 2.5%; legend text dark grey #434343 Inter Bold. Footnotes small Inter Regular dark grey #414142. Palette: navy #1A0A66, light orange #FBAD41, orange #F6821F, crimson #9B0D41, blue #3E74FF. Flat rectangles with no shadows or rounded corners; bottom gradient strip from #FBAD41 through #F6821F, red #C8102E, purple to blue #3E74FF.

Strong Cash Position
Issued 2026 Notes: Raised $790 million in our Convertible Note Offering due 2026 that priced on August 10, 2021, net of the repurchase of 2025 notes, issuance costs, and a capped call with respect to the 2026 convertible notes. The Notes will mature on August 15, 2026.

**Layout**

Title at top left. A financial table fills the lower 75%: row labels right-aligned in a left column ending near x 20%, four year columns (2022-2025) centered between x 22% and 57%, then two rounded-rectangle columns (Prior and Updated Long-Term Model) at the right. Cloudflare logo at top right, copyright footer bottom left, page number bottom right.

**Visuals**

Cloudflare cloud logo with CLOUDFLARE wordmark in white at top right. A rounded outlined panel with dark navy-to-transparent fill highlights the 2025 column, with an orange stroke. Two tall rounded rectangles with orange outlines and navy-to-red gradient fill hold the Prior and Updated Long-Term Model columns. Thin orange horizontal divider lines separate the header and each of the six rows across the table and panels. Two orange chevron-shaped lines (open arrows pointing right) link the 2025 column to the Prior column and the Prior column to the Updated column. A thin white horizontal line near the bottom runs from the left edge to about x 70% with a glowing white-orange light flare at its right end, over an orange glow in the bottom right corner.

**Design**

Background: Diagonal gradient from navy #1A0A6B (top left) via crimson #B5143A to orange #F97D1E (bottom right). Diagonal gradient background from deep navy-indigo at top left through magenta-purple and crimson red to bright orange at bottom right. Title white Inter SemiBold about 5.4% of slide height. Row labels white Inter Bold with parenthetical lines in Inter Regular, about 2.7%. Year headers in peach #FFD9A5 Inter Bold. Historical values in white Inter Regular. Prior column text light grey #DFDFDF Inter Bold, Updated column text white Inter Bold. Orange #F97316 lines, about 2px. Panels have large corner radii of about 3% of slide width. Footer text is tiny white Inter Regular.

### Slide 48

**Text**

Appendix
Definitions
Paying Customers. We believe our ability to grow the number of paying customers on our network provides a key indicator of growth of our business and our future business opportunities. We define a paying customer at the end of the quarter as a person or entity who has generated revenue and has an active contract with us or one of our partners during such quarter, excluding (i) customers that were not acquired through ordinary sales channels, (ii) customers using only our registrar product, and (iii) customers using our consumer applications, such as 1.1.1.1 DNS and 1.1.1.1 with WARP, which agreements and customers together represent an insignificant amount of our revenue. An entity is defined as a company, a government institution, a non-profit organization, or a distinct business unit of a large company. An active contract is defined as a customer relationship for which we have provided services during the quarter.
Paying Customers (> $100,000 Annualized Revenue). While we continue to grow customers across all sizes, over time, our large customers have contributed an increasing share of our revenue. We view the number of our paying customers with Annualized Revenue greater than $100,000 as indicative of our penetration within large enterprise accounts. To measure Annualized Revenue at the end of a quarter, we take the sum of revenue for each paying customer in the quarter and multiply that amount by four. For example, if we signed a new paying customer that generated $1,800 of revenue in a quarter, that customer would account for $7,200 of Annualized Revenue for that year. Our Annualized Revenue calculation excludes (i) agreements that were not entered into through ordinary sales channels, (ii) revenue generated from customers using only our registrar product, and (iii) customers using our consumer applications, such as 1.1.1.1 DNS and 1.1.1.1 with WARP, which agreements and customers together represent an insignificant amount of our revenue. Our Annualized Revenue metric also includes any usage charges by a customer during a period. As a result, Annualized Revenue may be higher than actual revenue over the course of the year.
Dollar-Based Net Retention Rate. Our ability to maintain long-term revenue growth and achieve profitability is dependent on our ability to retain and grow revenue generated from our existing paying customers. We believe that we will achieve these objectives by continuing to focus on customer loyalty and adding additional products and functionality to our network. Our dollar-based net retention rate is a key way we measure our performance in these areas. Dollar-based net retention rate measures our ability to retain and expand recurring revenue from existing customers. To calculate dollar-based net retention rate for a quarter, we compare the Annualized Revenue from paying customers four quarters prior to the Annualized Revenue from the same set of customers in the most recent quarter. Our dollar-based net retention rate includes expansion and is net of contraction and attrition, but excludes Annualized Revenue from new customers in the current period. Our dollar-based net retention rate excludes professional services and the benefit of free customers that upgrade to a paid subscription between the prior and current periods, even though this is an important source of incremental growth. We believe this provides a more meaningful representation of our ability to add incremental business from existing paying customers as they renew and expand their contracts.
Total Addressable Market. Total Addressable Market figures are Cloudflare estimates based on Gartner market forecasts and include the following Gartner sources: (1) Gartner, Forecast: Enterprise Network Equipment, Worldwide, 2024-2030, 1Q26, Vivek Tiwari, Christian Canales, Naresh Singh, 3/27/2026 (2) Gartner, Forecast Analysis: Edge Distribution Platforms, Worldwide, 2025, Brandon Medford, Jimmy Chuang, 1/26/2026 (3) Gartner, Forecast: Information Security, Worldwide, 2024-2030, 1Q26, Shailendra Upadhyay, Christian Canales, Ruggero Contu, Frank Marsala, Rahul Yadav, 3/26/2026 (4) Gartner, Forecast: IT Operations Management Software, Worldwide, 2022-2028, 2Q24 Update, Shailendra Upadhyay, Kanchi Bindal, Colin Fletcher, 10/16/2024 (5) Gartner, Forecast: Communications Services, Worldwide, 2024-2030, 1Q26, Grigory Betskov, Gaspar Valdivia, Khurram Shahzad, 3/26/2026 (6) Gartner, Forecast Analysis: Communications Platform as a Service, Worldwide, Lisa Unden-Farboud, Arunasree Cheparthi, 6/6/2025 (7) Gartner, Forecast: Enterprise Infrastructure Software, Worldwide, 2024-2030, 1Q26, Arunasree Cheparthi, Lisa Unden-Farboud, Robin Schumacher, Colin Fletcher, Sharat Menon, Kelli Smith, Saikat Ray, Nicholas Carter, Arthur Villa, Christian Canales, Brandon Medford, Amarendra ., Shailendra Upadhyay, 3/25/2026 (8) Gartner, Forecast: AI Spending, Worldwide, 2024-2029, 4Q25, Kay Arnott, Amarendra ., Adrian O'Connell, Hardeep Singh, Arunasree Cheparthi, Colleen Graham, Ranjit Atwal, Naresh Singh, Shailendra Upadhyay, Jon Erensen, Peter Middleton, 12/19/2025 (9) Gartner, Forecast Analysis: AI-Optimized IaaS, Hardeep Singh, Colleen Graham, 7/15/2025.
